HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000679/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_0fadf8a2b7de5d22d929","text":"3. **Untouched food/provision withheld only temporarily.** Evidence: س ج و B004 (not touching/taking food), ض ح و B003 in 93:1 (forenoon meal/grazing), ع ط و B002 and ر ض و B001 in 93:5 (giving and satisfaction). Structural cue: food-related branches flank the night and then resolve in future giving. Mechanism: the packet supplies \"not touching food\" and later giving; I supply the temporal relation that night pauses access without cancelling provision. Abductive move: interruption of intake is not the same as refusal. Reading change: \"when it settles\" can be heard as a closed interval before giving, not as deprivation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001392/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_55312c250de84a904fb5","text":"3. **Night remains a traversable medium.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ل ي ل:B002` includes entering or traveling by night; 93:7 supplies loss of location through `ض ل ل:B003` and gentle direction through `ه د ي:B001`. **Structural cue:** a scene of still night is eventually paired with a lost/found directional correction. **Assigned role:** the night branch supplies the medium of passage, while guidance supplies course. **Abductive move:** I infer that environmental stillness can coexist with the addressee's passage through uncertainty; the packet supplies no literal journey. **Reading change:** the oath can frame darkness as an interval navigated under guidance, not merely endured in immobility.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001392/B001","root_001635/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_6165913a3421de803ea7","text":"1. **Settled night is a pause, not relational cessation.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ل ي ل:B001` supplies night and its darkness, and fixed `س ج و:B001` supplies still settling and closing-in. **Structural cue:** the next ayah negates leaving (`و د ع:B001`) and aversion (`ق ل ي:B003`), while 93:5–7 show giving, shelter, and guidance. **Assigned role:** night supplies sensory non-visibility; the later verbs supply continuing agency despite it. **Abductive move:** the packet gives stillness followed by active care; I infer that lack of perceptible motion ≠ lack of relation. **Reading change:** `سَجَىٰ` becomes a bounded suspension within care rather than evidence that care has ended.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000679/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_641e1813f50b9147aef6","text":"4. **Native disposition as calm bearing.** Evidence: س ج و B003 (native disposition/character), ه د ي B010 in 93:7 (calm stillness and composed bearing), ر ض و B001 in 93:5 (acceptance opposed to displeasure). Structural cue: the calm night appears before the addressee is promised satisfaction and described as guided. Mechanism: the packet supplies disposition/calm branches; I supply the interiorization that cosmic stillness models a composure that the addressee will receive. Abductive move: outer night teaches inner steadiness. Reading change: the ayah can be read as forming character, not only describing time.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000679/B002","root_001392/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_671fab5b2826e9a206bd","text":"93:6 corrected the potentially terminal shroud-image of `س ج و:B002`: later shelter made protective covering more coherent than final closure. Then 93:7 activated `ل ي ل:B002` unexpectedly by pairing loss of location with guidance, making the settled night a medium through which direction can still be recovered.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000679/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9ca1aa6756559247b3a4","text":"2. **Shroud-image that raises the abandonment question.** Evidence: س ج و B002 (covering/shrouding with cloth), و د ع B003 in 93:3 (farewell including living or dead person), ق ل ي B003 in 93:3 (aversion casting out of heart). Structural cue: \"when it sajā\" is adjacent to \"has not taken farewell / has not hated.\" Mechanism: the packet supplies shrouding and farewell branches; I supply the affective fear that a covered-over silence could look like deathlike leave-taking. Abductive move: the verse lets the darkest possible inference surface so 93:3 can negate it. Reading change: night becomes an image of feared abandonment that is then explicitly cancelled.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000679/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_b7254fc63a33e790f847","text":"Later ayat forced a distinction between merciful quiet and harmful silencing. 93:10's لا تنهر made speech ethics central, so the night's س ج و B001 could not be generalized into \"silence is always good\"; the asker must not be silenced. 93:11 then made speech the final act, turning the night into a temporary cover before disclosure rather than a permanent concealment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000679/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c6735b78e164f4933d98","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:2:3\",\"root_000679\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Örtü çağrışımı önce son bir kefen gibi algılanabilse de sonraki barındırma ve yol gösterme, onu koruyan ve yeniden yönelmeye hazırlayan bir örtüye dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000070/B001","root_000679/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d662bc35784976c5dfc2","text":"2. **The cover can protect rather than shroud finally.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `س ج و:B002` is covering or shrouding with cloth; 93:6 activates `ء و ي:B001`, drawing into shelter, and 93:7 activates `ض ل ل:B002`, hiddenness, before `ه د ي:B001`, guidance. **Structural cue:** the cover-image is followed by a sheltering reversal and by movement from hiddenness toward direction. **Assigned role:** the covering branch conceals and encloses; shelter determines its protective rather than terminal function. **Abductive move:** the packet also permits the darker shroud image, but I infer cover → protection because the explicit trajectory continues into finding and guidance. **Reading change:** night may be heard as a refuge-like covering whose concealment preserves the covered person for reorientation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000679/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_daa7ff6baad3cd5c3a37","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:2:3\",\"root_000679\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Gecenin doğuştan huy ve karakter çağrışımı, dış dinginliği yol gösterilmeyle kazanılan iç sükunete örnek saymayı önerir; bu kişilik aktarımı oldukça dolaylıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000679/B001","root_001392/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e4a161502dda6784c78b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:2:1\",\"root_001392\",[\"B001\"]],[\"93:2:3\",\"root_000679\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Çöken gecenin dinginliği, ilişkinin kesilmesi değil, görünür hareket olmadan da bakımın sürdüğü sınırlı bir duraklama olarak belirginleşir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000679/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f809a85b61deeeec97ce","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:2:3\",\"root_000679\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Yemeğe el sürmeme çağrışımı, geceyi rızkın reddi değil, sonraki verişten önce erişimin geçici olarak durduğu bir aralık gibi düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001392/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_fbbe77757ba64874028c","text":"1. **Night as settled cover, not withdrawal.** Evidence: ل ي ل B001 (night opposite day and darkness), س ج و B001 (still settling/closing-in), و د ع B002 in 93:3 (restful ease), ر ب ب B007 in 93:3/5/11 (staying, clinging, lasting). Structural cue: night follows duha and immediately precedes the denial of being left. Mechanism: the packet supplies stillness and later non-abandonment; I supply the reversal that quiet darkness can be protective rest rather than absence. Abductive move: stillness is interpreted through ربّ-continuity as sustained care. Reading change: the ayah reads less like a contrast of light and dark, more like an argument that settled quiet is part of providence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001392/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ffdb4f01455f56f10880","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:2:1\",\"root_001392\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Geceleyin yol alma boyutu, karanlığı yalnız beklenen değil, yönün yeniden bulunabildiği bir geçiş ortamı yapar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
